# hono

_seed `1` · spec `eedd3b34a63e6293` · schema `0.1`_

## Phonology

- Consonants: p t k m n s l r w h
- Vowels: a e i o u
- Syllable shapes: `CV`x3, `CVC`x2

## Grammar sketch

- Slot order: plural > diminutive > augmentative > past > future > progressive > perfect > comparative > superlative > ordinal
- Inflection classes: noun-I, noun-II, verb-I, verb-II
  - noun-I: PL (ni), DIM (le), AUG (lu)
  - noun-II: PL (ni), DIM (wa), AUG (ta)
  - verb-I: PST (rut), FUT (ha), PROG (lop), PERF (mu)
  - verb-II: PST (mi), FUT (wuh), PROG (lop), PERF (koh)
- `ni` (PL, suffix on noun, noun-I)
- `ni` (PL, suffix on noun, noun-II)
- `le` (DIM, suffix on noun, noun-I)
- `wa` (DIM, suffix on noun, noun-II)
- `lu` (AUG, suffix on noun, noun-I)
- `ta` (AUG, suffix on noun, noun-II)
- `rut` (PST, suffix on verb, cop, verb-I)
- `mi` (PST, suffix on verb, cop, verb-II)
- `ha` (FUT, prefix on verb, cop, verb-I)
- `wuh` (FUT, prefix on verb, cop, verb-II)
- `lop` (PROG, suffix on verb, cop, verb-I)
- `lop` (PROG, suffix on verb, cop, verb-II)
- `mu` (PERF, suffix on verb, cop, verb-I)
- `koh` (PERF, suffix on verb, cop, verb-II)
- `kow` (ORD, suffix on num)
- `se` (ERG, suffix on noun)
- `wiw` (ABS, suffix on noun)
- `nuk` (3SG, suffix on verb, cop)
- Prepositions: above (mukmenup), across (hohe), against (kirsulok), along (rihnemet), at (horle), behind (pisapmom), below (puwuh), beside (kultata), between (kik), from (powwan), in (wumnorpi), into (piwu), on (tiluso), out of (ta), over (lo), through (tinusem), to (kulam), toward (niw), under (homip), with (tapa)
- Copula: be (husew)
- Conjunctions: and (masalek), but (kilpow), so (salener), then (mupe)

## Syntax

- Word order: SVO
- Alignment: ergative
- Modifier order: head-initial (modifiers after the head)
- Noun phrase: re nomirte - "water big"
- Example: rese wowleruknuk rinewiw - "water.ERG say.3SG fire.ABS"
- Example (intransitive): rewiw wowleruknuk - "water.ABS say.3SG"
- Example (predicate adjective): rewiw husewnuk nomirte - "water.ABS be.3SG big"
- Example (two clauses): rese wowleruknuk rinewiw masalek rinese wowleruknuk rewiw - "water.ERG say.3SG fire.ABS and fire.ERG say.3SG water.ABS"
